add_namespace = int_phalanstere_events
add_namespace = fcf_international_events
add_namespace = fcf_diplomacy_events

###法伦施泰尔
	country_event = {
		id = int_phalanstere_events.1
		title = int_phalanstere_events.1.title
		desc = int_phalanstere_events.1.desc
		is_triggered_only = yes
		option = {
			name = int_phalanstere_events.1.a
			effect_tooltip = { add_one_random_infrastructure = yes }
			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = tooltip_modify_dynmod
				DYNMOD = FCF_international_phalanstere_modifier
			}
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_to_variable = {
					international_phalanstere_modifier_production_speed_infrastructure_factor = 0.15
					tooltip = production_speed_infrastructure_factor_tt
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					international_phalanstere_modifier_production_speed_rail_way_factor = 0.25
					tooltip = production_speed_rail_way_factor_tt
				}
			}
			ai_chance = { base = 10 }
		}
	}

	country_event = {
		id = int_phalanstere_events.9
		title = int_phalanstere_events.9.title
		desc = int_phalanstere_events.9.desc
		is_triggered_only = yes
		option = {
			name = int_phalanstere_events.9.a
			effect_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						capital_scope = {
							land_facility < 1
							can_construct_building = land_facility
						}
					}
					capital_scope = { construct_building_in_random_province = { land_facility = 1 } }
				}
				else = {
					add_breakthrough_progress = {
						specialization = specialization_land
						value = 1.5
					}
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = tooltip_modify_dynmod
				DYNMOD = FCF_international_phalanstere_modifier
			}
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_to_variable = {
					international_phalanstere_modifier_research_speed_factor = 0.05
					tooltip = research_speed_factor_tt
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					international_phalanstere_modifier_specialization_land_speed_factor = 0.1
					tooltip = specialization_land_speed_factor_tt
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					international_phalanstere_modifier_specialization_air_speed_factor = 0.1
					tooltip = specialization_air_speed_factor_tt
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					international_phalanstere_modifier_specialization_naval_speed_factor = 0.1
					tooltip = specialization_naval_speed_factor_tt
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					international_phalanstere_modifier_specialization_nuclear_speed_factor = 0.1
					tooltip = specialization_nuclear_speed_factor_tt
				}
			}
			ai_chance = { base = 10 }
		}
	}

	country_event = {
		id = int_phalanstere_events.102
		title = int_phalanstere_events.102.title
		desc = int_phalanstere_events.102.desc
		is_triggered_only = yes
		option = {
			name = int_phalanstere_events.102.a
			trigger = { any_owned_state = { infrastructure < 5 } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = FCF_international_phalanstere_invest_infrastructure days = 120 }

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = if_phalanstere_has_agreed_about_what
				COOPERATION = phalanstere_cooperation_infrastructure
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = shorten_invest_time_by_60_days
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_global_flag = phalanstere_cooperation_infrastructure }
					modify_timed_idea = { idea = FCF_international_phalanstere_invest_infrastructure days = -60 }
				}
			}
			ai_chance = { base = 10 }
		}
		option = {
			name = int_phalanstere_events.102.b
			trigger = { any_owned_state = { can_construct_building = industrial_complex } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = FCF_international_phalanstere_invest_civilian days = 120 }

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = if_phalanstere_has_agreed_about_what
				COOPERATION = phalanstere_cooperation_complex
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = shorten_invest_time_by_60_days
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_global_flag = phalanstere_cooperation_complex }
					modify_timed_idea = { idea = FCF_international_phalanstere_invest_civilian days = -60 }
				}
			}
			ai_chance = { base = 10 }
		}
		option = {
			name = int_phalanstere_events.102.c
			trigger = { any_owned_state = { can_construct_building = arms_factory } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = FCF_international_phalanstere_invest_military days = 120 }

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = if_phalanstere_has_agreed_about_what
				COOPERATION = phalanstere_cooperation_arms
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = shorten_invest_time_by_60_days
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_global_flag = phalanstere_cooperation_arms }
					modify_timed_idea = { idea = FCF_international_phalanstere_invest_military days = -60 }
				}
			}
			ai_chance = { base = 10 }
		}
		option = {
			name = int_phalanstere_events.102.d
			trigger = {
				any_owned_state = {
					OR = {
						aluminium > 0
						chromium > 0
						oil > 0
						rubber > 0
						steel > 0
						tungsten > 0
						coal > 0
					}
				}
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = FCF_international_phalanstere_invest_resource days = 120 }

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = if_phalanstere_has_agreed_about_what
				COOPERATION = phalanstere_cooperation_resource
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = shorten_invest_time_by_60_days
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_global_flag = phalanstere_cooperation_resource }
					modify_timed_idea = { idea = FCF_international_phalanstere_invest_resource days = -60 }
				}
			}
			ai_chance = { base = 10 }
		}
	}
	country_event = {
		id = int_phalanstere_events.103
		title = int_phalanstere_events.103.title
		desc = int_phalanstere_events.103.desc
		is_triggered_only = yes
		option = {
			name = int_phalanstere_events.103.a
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						any_other_country = {
							FRA_is_phalanstere_member = yes
							has_tech = construction1
						}
						ROOT = { NOT = { has_tech = construction1 } }
					}
					AND = {
						any_other_country = {
							FRA_is_phalanstere_member = yes
							has_tech = construction2
						}
						ROOT = { NOT = { has_tech = construction2 } }
					}
					AND = {

country_event = {
	id = fcf_international_events.4
	title = fcf_international_events.4.title
	desc = {
		text = fcf_international_events.4.desc.local
		trigger = { has_country_flag = local_revolution_organization }
	}
	desc = {
		text = fcf_international_events.4.desc.int
		trigger = { has_country_flag = international_revolution_organization }
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.4.a
		international_intervention_countermeasures_activate = yes

		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = local_revolution_organization }
			set_variable = { ii_needed_days = 60 }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { party_popularity_100@totalist > party_popularity_100@syndicalist }
					check_variable = { party_popularity_100@totalist > party_popularity_100@radical_socialist }
				}
				add_popularity = { ideology = totalist popularity = 0.03 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { party_popularity_100@syndicalist > party_popularity_100@totalist }
					check_variable = { party_popularity_100@syndicalist > party_popularity_100@radical_socialist }
				}
				add_popularity = { ideology = syndicalist popularity = 0.03 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { party_popularity_100@radical_socialist > party_popularity_100@syndicalist }
					check_variable = { party_popularity_100@radical_socialist > party_popularity_100@totalist }
				}
				add_popularity = { ideology = radical_socialist popularity = 0.03 }
			}
			else = {
				add_popularity = { ideology = totalist popularity = 0.01 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = syndicalist popularity = 0.01 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = radical_socialist popularity = 0.01 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = international_revolution_organization }
			set_variable = { ii_needed_days = 40 }

			if = {
				limit = { phalanstere_totalist_advantage = yes }
				add_popularity = { ideology = totalist popularity = 0.03 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { phalanstere_syndicalist_advantage = yes }
				add_popularity = { ideology = syndicalist popularity = 0.03 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { phalanstere_radical_socialist_advantage = yes }
				add_popularity = { ideology = radical_socialist popularity = 0.03 }
			}
			else = {
				add_popularity = { ideology = totalist popularity = 0.01 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = syndicalist popularity = 0.01 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = radical_socialist popularity = 0.01 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

		}

		}
		else = { add_ideas = FCF_international_CAN_QUE_strike_stage_2 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}

	}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_international_events.40
	title = {
		text = fcf_international_events.40.title.FCF
		trigger = { COR = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result > 3 } } }
	}
	title = {
		text = fcf_international_events.40.title.NFA
		trigger = { COR = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result < -3 } } }
	}
	title = {
		text = fcf_international_events.40.title.COR
		trigger = {
			COR = { 
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result < -3 } 
					check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result > 3 }
				} 
			} 
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = fcf_international_events.40.desc.FCF
		trigger = { COR = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result > 3 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = fcf_international_events.40.desc.NFA
		trigger = { COR = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result < -3 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = fcf_international_events.40.desc.COR
		trigger = {
			COR = { 
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result < -3 } 
					check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result > 3 }
				} 
			} 
		}
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.40.a
		trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { COR = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result > 3 } } }
				tag = FRA
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { COR = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result < -3 } } }
				tag = NFA
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { tag = FRA }
				annex_country = { target = COR transfer_troops = yes } 
				1 = {
					hidden_effect = { add_core_of = FRA }
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = state_strategy_take_what
						ZONE = local_zone
						STATE_STRATEGY = state_strategy_state_of_mafia
					}
					state_strategy_37_get = yes
					add_to_array = { array = FCF_state_strategy value = 37 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				annex_country = { target = COR transfer_troops = yes } 
				add_ideas = FCF_NFA_winner_of_state_of_mafia
				1 = { 
					hidden_effect = { add_core_of = NFA }
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = FCF_international_state_state_of_mafia_modifier } 
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.40.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				if = {
					limit = { COR = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result > 3 } } }
					tag = NFA
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { COR = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result < -3 } } }
					tag = FRA
				}

				COR = { 
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result < -3 } 
						check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result > 3 }
					} 
				} 
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { tag = FRA }
				NFA = { annex_country = { target = COR transfer_troops = yes } }
			}
			else = { FRA = { annex_country = { target = COR transfer_troops = yes } } }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.40.COR
		trigger = { tag = COR }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result > 3 } } 
			FRA = { 
				annex_country = { target = COR transfer_troops = yes } 
				1 = {
					remove_core_of = COR
					add_core_of = FRA 
					custom_effect_tooltip = {
						localization_key = state_strategy_take_what
						ZONE = local_zone
						STATE_STRATEGY = state_strategy_state_of_mafia
					}
					state_strategy_37_get = yes
					add_to_array = { array = FCF_state_strategy value = 37 }
				}
				country_event = { id = fcf_society_events.8 days = 7 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { state_of_mafia_result < -3 } } 
			NFA = { 
				annex_country = { target = COR transfer_troops = yes } 
				add_ideas = FCF_NFA_winner_of_state_of_mafia
				1 = { 
					remove_core_of = COR
					add_core_of = NFA 
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = FCF_international_state_state_of_mafia_modifier } 
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

						state = 679
					}

				ott_increase_revolt_risk = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { FRA = { check_variable = { economy_power > 150 } } }
			hidden_effect = { FRA = { add_to_variable = { economy_power = -150 } } }
			add_political_power = -50
			subtract_from_variable = { syr_integration_factor = 5 tooltip = ott_decrease_syr_int }
			subtract_from_variable = { kur_integration_factor = 5 tooltip = ott_decrease_kur_int }
			subtract_from_variable = { hej_integration_factor = 5 tooltip = ott_decrease_hej_int }
			subtract_from_variable = { irq_integration_factor = 5 tooltip = ott_decrease_irq_int }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.119.a
		add_political_power = -25
		add_stability = -0.05
		add_popularity = { ideology = radical_socialist popularity = 0.05 }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						set_temp_variable = { instability_chance_weight = 0.5 }
						subtract_from_temp_variable = { instability_chance_weight = THIS.stability }
						multiply_temp_variable = { instability_chance_weight = 100 }
						add = instability_chance_weight
					}
					modifier = {
						set_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_chance_weight = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@syndicalist }
						add_to_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_chance_weight = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@totalist }
						add_to_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_chance_weight = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@radical_socialist }
						add = socialist_popularity_chance_weight
					}
					country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.120 days = 2 random_hours = 12 }
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						set_temp_variable = { stability_chance_weight = 0.5 }
						subtract_from_temp_variable = { stability_chance_weight = THIS.stability }
						multiply_temp_variable = { stability_chance_weight = -100 }
						add = stability_chance_weight
					}
					modifier = {
						set_temp_variable = { non_socialist_popularity_chance_weight = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@syndicalist }
						add_to_temp_variable = { non_socialist_popularity_chance_weight = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@totalist }
						add_to_temp_variable = { non_socialist_popularity_chance_weight = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@radical_socialist }
						multiply_temp_variable = { non_socialist_popularity_chance_weight = -1 }
						add = non_socialist_popularity_chance_weight
					}
					country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.121 days = 2 random_hours = 12 }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

		MAF_territory_randomly_pick = yes
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = change_num }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = 0 }

country_event = {
	id = fcf_international_events.163
	title = fcf_international_events.163.title
	desc = fcf_international_events.163.desc

	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.163.a
		add_stability = -0.1
		set_country_flag = ii_has_finished
		clr_country_flag = FCF_II_HAD
		international_intervention_revolution_feasibility_update = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				var:THIS.ii_revolution_feasibility = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_war_support > 0.5 }
						country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.167 days = 5 }
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_war_support > 0.2 }
						country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.168 days = 5 }
					}
					else = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									AND = {
										OR = {
											has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_republic
											has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_democrat
										}
										set_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@syndicalist }
										add_to_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@totalist }
										add_to_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@radical_socialist }

										set_temp_variable = { democratic_popularity_weighted = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@social_democrat }
										add_to_temp_variable = { democratic_popularity_weighted = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@social_liberal }
										multiply_temp_variable = { democratic_popularity_weighted = 2 }

										check_variable = { socialist_popularity_total > democratic_popularity_weighted }
									}

									NOT = {
										has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_republic
										has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_democrat
									}
								}
							}
							country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.165 days = 5 }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_republic
									has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_democrat
								}
							}
							country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.164 days = 5 }
						}
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						set_temp_variable = { revolution_failure_weight = ii_revolution_feasibility }
						multiply_temp_variable = { revolution_failure_weight = -1 }
						add = revolution_failure_weight
					}
					country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.166 days = 7 }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

		name = fcf_international_events.167.a

				is_in_faction = yes
				is_subject = no
			}
			set_variable = { THIS.ii_previous_faction_leader = faction_leader }
			leave_faction = yes
			peace_out_non_neighbours_after_leaving_faction = yes
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				add_named_threat = {
					threat = 3
					name = [TAG][LOC]
				}
			}
			TAG = "[?THIS.GetName]"
			LOC = "[civil_war_threat_loc]"
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = ii_civil_war_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_republic
						has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_democrat
					}
					set_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@syndicalist }
					add_to_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@totalist }
					add_to_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@radical_socialist }

					set_temp_variable = { democratic_popularity_weighted = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@social_democrat }
					add_to_temp_variable = { democratic_popularity_weighted = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@social_liberal }
					multiply_temp_variable = { democratic_popularity_weighted = 2 }

					check_variable = { socialist_popularity_total < democratic_popularity_weighted }
				}
				set_political_party = { ideology = totalist popularity = 0 }
				set_political_party = { ideology = syndicalist popularity = 0 }
				set_political_party = { ideology = radical_socialist popularity = 0 }
				set_political_party = { ideology = social_democrat popularity = 0 }
				set_political_party = { ideology = social_liberal popularity = 0 }
				international_intervention_non_socialist_war_evaluate = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_political_party = { ideology = totalist popularity = 0 }
				set_political_party = { ideology = syndicalist popularity = 0 }
				set_political_party = { ideology = radical_socialist popularity = 0 }
				international_intervention_socialist_war_evaluate = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

						limit = { check_variable = { ii_ire_revolution_army > 4 } }

					}

				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						set_temp_variable = { revolution_failure_weight = ii_revolution_feasibility }
						multiply_temp_variable = { revolution_failure_weight = -1 }
						add = revolution_failure_weight
					}
					country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.180 days = 6 }
					FRA = { country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.180 days = 6 } }
					ENG = { country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.180 days = 6 } }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = IRE_had_govt_blueshirt_coup }
					set_country_flag = { flag = IRE_gen_EOD_to_syndie days = 20 value = 1 }
				}
				else = { set_country_flag = { flag = IRE_gen_SF_to_syndie days = 20 value = 1 } }
				country_event = { id = ireland_events.42 days = 5 }
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = totalist
					elections_allowed = no
				}
				set_temp_variable = { coalition_partner_var = token:syndicalist }
				add_to_coalition = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = IRE_had_govt_blueshirt_coup }
					set_country_flag = { flag = IRE_blueshirt_to_totalist days = 5 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = IRE_polpath_se }
					}
					set_country_flag = IRE_polpath_se
				}
				IRE_remove_recorded_govt = yes
				set_country_flag = IRE_govt_se
				IRE_load_socialist_govt_advisors = yes
				activate_advisor = IRE_george_gilmore_sic
				IRE_dail_eireann = { remove_second_in_command_role = yes }
				set_variable = { IRE_legitimacy = 50 }
				set_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@syndicalist }
				add_to_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@totalist }
				add_to_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@radical_socialist }
				set_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_remainder = socialist_popularity_total }
				modulo_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_remainder = 10 }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = socialist_popularity_remainder }
				multiply_temp_variable = { socialist_popularity_total = 0.5 }
				add_to_variable = { IRE_legitimacy = socialist_popularity_total }
				set_variable = { IRE_legitimacy_cost = 5 }
				set_variable = { IRE_legitimacy_cost_small = 3 }
				country_event = { id = ireland_events.3525 days = 3 }
				uncomplete_national_focus = {
					focus = IRE_labour
					uncomplete_children = yes
				}
				uncomplete_national_focus = {
					focus = IRE_sinn_fein
					uncomplete_children = yes
				}
				uncomplete_national_focus = {
					focus = IRE_saorstat_criostuil
					uncomplete_children = yes
				}
				uncomplete_national_focus = {
					focus = IRE_aontas_naisiunta
					uncomplete_children = yes
				}
				uncomplete_national_focus = {
					focus = IRE_republic_maintained
					uncomplete_children = yes
				}
				GER_remove_from_mitteleuropa = yes
				news_event = fcf_international_events.188
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

									add_to_temp_variable = { democratic_popularity_weighted = var:THIS.party_popularity_100@social_liberal }
									multiply_temp_variable = { democratic_popularity_weighted = 2 }

									check_variable = { socialist_popularity_total > democratic_popularity_weighted }
								}

								NOT = {
									has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_republic
									has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_democrat
								}

							OR = {
								has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_republic
								has_country_flag = ii_cooperate_with_democrat
							}
						}
						country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.164 days = 5 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						set_temp_variable = { revolution_failure_weight = ii_revolution_feasibility }
						multiply_temp_variable = { revolution_failure_weight = -1 }
						add = revolution_failure_weight
					}
					country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.166 days = 7 }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_international_events.251
	title = fcf_international_events.251.title
	desc = fcf_international_events.251.desc

	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.251.a
		hidden_effect = {
			international_intervention_revolution_feasibility_update = yes
			set_temp_variable = { intervention_success_weight = 0 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { intervention_success_weight = THIS.ii_revolution_feasibility }
			random_list = {
				var:THIS.intervention_success_weight = { country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.256 days = 7 } }
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						set_temp_variable = { intervention_failure_weight = intervention_success_weight }
						multiply_temp_variable = { intervention_failure_weight = -1 }
						add = intervention_failure_weight
					}
					country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.255 days = 7 }
					FRA = { country_event = { id = fcf_international_events.255 days = 7 } }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.251.b
		trigger = { is_in_faction = yes }
		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
				is_in_faction_with = SWI
			}
			country_event = fcf_international_events.252
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

		has_stability < 0.5
		NOT = { has_country_flag = economic_crisis_immigration_not_show }

	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.263.a
		international_economic_crisis_immigration_evaluate = yes

		international_economic_crisis_immigration_event_trigger = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { FRA = { has_country_flag = economic_crisis_immigration_not_show_fra } } }

		}
	}

	id = fcf_international_events.267
	title = fcf_international_events.267.title
	desc = fcf_international_events.267.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.267.a
		set_variable = { GER_economic_health = -6 }

		effect_tooltip = {
			add_to_variable = {
				MAF.MAF_colonial_report_adjustment = -56
				tooltip = MAF_colonial_report_reduce_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				GER.GER_MAF_credibility = 8
				tooltip = GER_MAF_credibility_improve_tt
			}
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = GER_MAF_colonial_illusion_activating_tt

		set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_true_cost_political_power_gain = -0.2 }
		set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_true_cost_consumer_goods_factor = 0.2 }
		set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_true_cost_industrial_factory_donations = -6 }
		set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_true_cost_unit_limit_bonus = -2 }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_colonial_illusion_true_cost_modifier } 
		
		set_variable = { colonial_illusion_true_cost_political_power_gain = -0.2 }
		set_variable = { colonial_illusion_true_cost_consumer_goods_factor = 0.2 }
		set_variable = { colonial_illusion_true_cost_industrial_factory_donations = -6 }
		set_variable = { colonial_illusion_true_cost_unit_limit_bonus = -2 }
		
		set_variable = { MAF_construction_points_monthly = 1 }
		set_variable = { MAF_suppress_points_monthly = 2 }
		set_variable = { GER_colonial_troops_scale = 0 }

		hidden_effect = { MAF_colonial_illusion_modifier_update = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_international_events.268
	title = fcf_international_events.268.title
	desc = fcf_international_events.268.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.268.a
		add_to_variable = {
			MAF_true_stability = -7
			tooltip = MAF_true_stability_change_tt
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAF_true_stability_below_0_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line

		#德属东非
		#出口农业受挫，但本地农业仍能维持基本经济活动
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 965 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -6 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#喀麦隆保护领
		#可可、橡胶和种植园出口高度依赖德国市场
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 927 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -2 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -7 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#刚果保护领
		#矿业、特许公司和外来资本同时遭受冲击
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 295 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -3 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -10 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#加蓬保护领
		#木材出口下降，但当地货币经济规模有限
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 539 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -4 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#多哥保护领
		#经济规模较小，但对出口农业和德国贸易依赖较深
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 877 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -6 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#达荷美保护领
		#棕榈产品出口受损，自给农业缓冲了部分冲击
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 876 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -4 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#德属西南非洲
		#矿业、畜牧业和负债经营的定居农场受到严重打击
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 541 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -2 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -9 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#尼日利亚殖民地/保护领
		#出口规模庞大，但商品种类和内部市场提供了一定缓冲
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 973 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -6 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#黄金海岸殖民地
		#对可可出口高度依赖，是第一波危机中受创最重的地区之一
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 274 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -2 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -10 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#肯尼亚殖民地/保护领
		#定居农业依赖信贷、土地价格和欧洲出口市场
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 547 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -3 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -8 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#乌干达保护领
		#棉花出口受挫，但间接统治和本地农业维持了基本秩序
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 548 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -5 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#北罗得西亚
		#经济高度依赖铜矿，是受工业需求崩溃影响最大的地区
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 813 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -3 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -11 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		#尼亚萨兰保护领
		#出口经济规模有限，但劳务收入和殖民财政仍受到冲击
		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = 871 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = -3 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_international_events.269
	title = fcf_international_events.269.title
	desc = fcf_international_events.269.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.269.a
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { MAF_construction_points < MAF_territory_construction_cost^MAF_targeted_territory } } }
			set_temp_variable = { MAF_construction_points_change = { value = MAF_territory_construction_cost^MAF_targeted_territory multiply = -1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { MAF_suppress_points_change = 0 }
			MAF_construction_and_suppress_points_change = yes
		}
		else_if = { 
			limit = { check_variable = { MAF_construction_points > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { pp_cost = { value = MAF_construction_points subtract = MAF_territory_construction_cost^MAF_targeted_territory multiply = 10 } }
			add_political_power = pp_cost

			set_temp_variable = { MAF_construction_points_change = { value = MAF_construction_points multiply = -1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { MAF_suppress_points_change = 0 }
			MAF_construction_and_suppress_points_change = yes
		}
		else = { 
			set_temp_variable = { pp_cost = { value = -10 multiply = MAF_territory_construction_cost^MAF_targeted_territory } }
			add_political_power = pp_cost
		}

		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = MAF_targeted_territory }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_stability_change = 0 }
		set_temp_variable_to_random = { var = territory_economy_change max = improve_territory_economy_max min = improve_territory_economy_min }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		add_to_variable = { MAF_territory_construction_cost^MAF_targeted_territory = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = MAF_territory_construction_cost^MAF_targeted_territory max = 3 min = 1 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.269.b
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { MAF_suppress_points < MAF_territory_suppress_cost^MAF_targeted_territory } } }
			set_temp_variable = { MAF_construction_points_change = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { MAF_suppress_points_change = { value = MAF_territory_suppress_cost^MAF_targeted_territory multiply = -1 } }
			MAF_construction_and_suppress_points_change = yes
		}
		else_if = { 
			limit = { check_variable = { MAF_suppress_points > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { pp_cost = { value = MAF_suppress_points subtract = MAF_territory_suppress_cost^MAF_targeted_territory multiply = 10 } }
			add_political_power = pp_cost

			set_temp_variable = { MAF_construction_points_change = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { MAF_suppress_points_change = { value = MAF_suppress_points multiply = -1 } }
			MAF_construction_and_suppress_points_change = yes
		}
		else = { 
			set_temp_variable = { pp_cost = { value = -10 multiply = MAF_territory_suppress_cost^MAF_targeted_territory }  }
			add_political_power = pp_cost
		}

		set_temp_variable = { territory_id = MAF_targeted_territory }
		set_temp_variable_to_random = { var = territory_stability_change max = improve_territory_stability_max min = improve_territory_stability_min }
		set_temp_variable = { territory_economy_change = 0 }
		MAF_territory_stability_or_economy_change = yes

		add_to_variable = { MAF_territory_suppress_cost^MAF_targeted_territory = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = MAF_territory_suppress_cost^MAF_targeted_territory max = 3 min = 1 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.269.c

		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	after = { hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = MAF_dealing_with_local_situations_activating } }
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_international_events.270
	title = fcf_international_events.270.title
	desc = fcf_international_events.270.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.270.a
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = {
					localization_key = MAF_territory_name
					NAME = mittelafrika_territory_[ID]
				}
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = { check_variable = { mittelafrika_territory_capital_state = [ID] } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_demilitarized_zone = no }
						set_state_flag = MAF_demilitarized_zone
						set_demilitarized_zone = yes
					}
					else = {
						set_state_flag = MAF_not_cored_zone
						add_compliance = -20
						add_resistance = 30
					}
				}
			}
			ID = "[?MAF_targeted_territory]"
		}

		clr_country_flag = MAF_territory_crisis_event_flag
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_international_events.272
	title = fcf_international_events.272.title
	desc = fcf_international_events.272.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.272.a
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = GER_has_evacuate_africa }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_political_power_gain = -1.5 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_stability_factor = -0.6 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor = -0.45 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_industrial_capacity_factory = -0.45 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_production_speed_buildings_factor = -0.45 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_consumer_goods_factor = 0.75 }

			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_political_power_gain = -1.5 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_stability_factor = -0.6 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor = -0.45 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_industrial_capacity_factory = -0.45 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_production_speed_buildings_factor = -0.45 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_consumer_goods_factor = 0.75 }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_political_power_gain = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_stability_factor = -0.8 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor = -0.6 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_industrial_capacity_factory = -0.6 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_production_speed_buildings_factor = -0.6 }
			set_temp_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_consumer_goods_factor = 1 }

			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_political_power_gain = -2 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_stability_factor = -0.8 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor = -0.6 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_industrial_capacity_factory = -0.6 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_production_speed_buildings_factor = -0.6 }
			set_variable = { colonial_illusion_collapsed_consumer_goods_factor = 1 }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_colonial_illusion_collapsed_modifier }

		
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_international_events.281
	title = fcf_international_events.281.title
	desc = fcf_international_events.281.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_international_events.281.a
		add_stability = 0.1
		add_war_support = 0.2

		effect_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { MAF.MAF_colonial_report_adjustment < 30 } }
				add_to_variable = {
					MAF.MAF_colonial_report_adjustment = { value = 30 subtract = MAF.MAF_colonial_report_adjustment }
					tooltip = MAF_colonial_report_improve_tt
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				GER.GER_MAF_credibility = -100
				tooltip = GER_MAF_credibility_reduce_tt
			}
		}
		
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = MAF_colonial_illusion_true_stability_modifier }
		hidden_effect = { GER = { country_event = fcf_international_events.282 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

		add_war_support = 0.1

			limit = { check_variable = { MAF.MAF_colonial_report_adjustment < 30 } }

				MAF.MAF_colonial_report_adjustment = { value = 30 subtract = MAF.MAF_colonial_report_adjustment }
				tooltip = MAF_colonial_report_improve_tt
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			add_to_variable = {

			}
		}
